                          - generic [ref=e575]:
                            - paragraph [ref=e576]: "Weapon: Look for a high accuracy weapon, preferably with high crit chance or crit dmg. +lvl to proj skills is also good. Attack speed isn't important. Phys or lightning damage are equal. Preferable use a spear with a high crit chance base, seaglass base is the best at 10% base crit."
                            - paragraph [ref=e577]:
                              - generic [ref=e582]: Kitoko's Current
                              - text: are mandatory for this build to function, it's how you generate frenzy charges with
                              - generic [ref=e587]: Combat Frenzy
                              - text: . Without frenzies the build will feel significantly worse.
                            - paragraph [ref=e588]:
                              - generic [ref=e593]: Lycosidae
                              - text: is not needed but it is your best in slot shield, so buy it when you can afford it.
                            - paragraph [ref=e594]: You want the highest evasion chest you can afford, this is massive for your evasion which is your core defense.
                            - paragraph [ref=e595]: for rings you want flat lightning damage or phys dmg to attacks with life/resis.
                            - paragraph [ref=e596]:
                              - text: For your belt you want Str/life/resist.
                              - generic [ref=e601]: Headhunter
                              - text: or
                              - generic [ref=e606]: Ingenuity
                              - text: would be ideal if you can afford it.
                            - paragraph [ref=e607]:
                              - text: For your helmet you want Accuracy/life/resist. Down the line you can go a Pure ES helmet and grab
                              - generic [ref=e612]: Subterfuge Mask
                              - text: .
                            - paragraph [ref=e613]: Jewel or ring with mana on kill will solve your mana issues.

                          - generic [ref=e1055]:
                            - paragraph [ref=e1056]: This build requires at least 6 ascendancy points
                            - paragraph [ref=e1057]:
                              - text: You need Critical strike/penetrate/Elemental infusion.
                              - generic [ref=e1062]: Predatory Instinct
                              - text: is your last ascension.
                            - paragraph [ref=e1063]:

                      - generic [ref=e1064]:
                        - heading "How it Plays" [level=2] [ref=e1068]
                        - generic [ref=e1075]:
                          - paragraph [ref=e1076]:
                            - text: For clear you move around and throw lightning spears. Do not spam your
                            - generic [ref=e1081]: Lightning Spear
                            - text: as you only want to throw it if you have 10+
                            - generic [ref=e1086]: Volt
                            - text: stacks.
                          - paragraph [ref=e1087]:
                            - text: For single target you want to make sure you're always using
                            - generic [ref=e1092]: Barrage
                            - text: with
                            - generic [ref=e1097]: Storm Lance
                            - text: on cooldown. If you have a spare frenzy from
                            - generic [ref=e1102]: Sniper's Mark
                            - generic [ref=e1107]: Profusion
                            - text: ", then throw another non-barraged storm lance. It should look something like this."
                          - paragraph [ref=e1108]:
                            - text: pre-throw
                            - generic [ref=e1113]: Storm Lance
                            - text: ">"
                            - generic [ref=e1118]: Sniper's Mark
                            - text: ">"
                            - generic [ref=e1123]: Barrage
                            - text: ">"
                            - generic [ref=e1128]: Storm Lance
                            - text: (with frenzy).
                            - text: After that you're just rotating snipers mark/barrage/storm lance
                            - text: For higher single target dps, put
                            - generic [ref=e1133]: Profusion
                            - text: with
                            - generic [ref=e1138]: Sniper's Mark
                          - paragraph [ref=e1139]: For pinnacle bosses you can takeout combat frenzy and rhoa, this will give you enough spirit to run cast on crit, which will automate your snipers mark, making the rotation a lot easier.
                          - paragraph [ref=e1140]:
                            - generic [ref=e1145]: Cast on Critical
                            - text: /
                            - generic [ref=e1150]: Sniper's Mark
                            - text: /
                            - generic [ref=e1155]: Profusion
                            - text: /
                            - generic [ref=e1160]: Impetus
                            - text: /
                            - generic [ref=e1165]: Ingenuity
                            - text: "- This will be your setup for pinnacle bosses."
                          - paragraph [ref=e1166]:
                            - text: "Optional setup for more boss damage:"
                            - text: Have a weapon swap with
                            - generic [ref=e1171]: Effigy of Cruelty
                            - text: . Then have a
                            - generic [ref=e1176]: Orb of Storms
                            - text: with weapon swap 2, combined with
                            - generic [ref=e1181]: Inevitable Critical
                            - text: ","
                            - generic [ref=e1186]: Pinpoint Critical
                            - text: and
                            - generic [ref=e1191]: Font of Rage
                            - text: . This will give you a lot more crit on bosses, would only do this for pinnacle content. (I would also get a wand with cast speed on the weapon swap if you have the int)
                      - generic [ref=e1192]:
                        - heading "How it Works" [level=2] [ref=e1196]
                        - generic [ref=e1203]:
                          - heading "Quest rewards to take:" [level=3] [ref=e1204]
                          - paragraph [ref=e1205]:
                            - text: 1) Mana regen from flask
                            - text: 2) stun threshold from venom reward - act 3
                            - text: 3) Life recovery from flask
                            - text: 4) Chaos res from venom reward - act 6
                          - paragraph [ref=e1206]:
                            - strong [ref=e1207]: How do I generate/maintain frenzy charges?
                            - text: Frenzies are generated when you electrocute a monster.
                            - generic [ref=e1212]: Combat Frenzy
                            - text: will generate the frenzy and then
                            - generic [ref=e1217]: Storm Lance
                            - text: or
                            - generic [ref=e1222]: Lightning Spear
                            - text: will consume it. This is why you don't want to spam
                            - generic [ref=e1227]: Lightning Spear
                            - text: while clearing and only use it with 10-20+
                            - generic [ref=e1232]: Volt
                            - text: charges.
                          - paragraph [ref=e1233]:
                            - text: Make sure your combat frenzy is qualitied and has
                            - generic [ref=e1238]: Profusion
                            - text: in the links.
